# Full-row package benchmark comparison

Timing units are nanoseconds. p50 is the median of block medians; p95 is the sorted retained sample at index floor(0.95 × (N − 1)). All retained samples are included. Status, p, and 95% CI come from the preserved Labs comparison output. Neutral is Labs’ statistical classification using its 5% repository minimum delta; it is not a user admission threshold.
